## 2026-08-02 — ENG-P2-001-01: Identity Domain Foundation (application code, TDD)

- **Context:** following the Founder-authorized merge of `ENG-P2-GATE-001` (PR #56, merge commit `718dde601b665ad42bd649e0183313cae57644cc`), the Founder requested implementation of `ENG-P2-001-01` — the Customer Identity domain-foundation layer defined by `ENG-P2-ARCH-001` and `ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001`. **This is the first application-code task in this session's Identity work** — governed by the repository's binding test-driven-development discipline.
- **Implemented at `functions/src/domains/identity/`:** the `CustomerIdentity` aggregate root (registration, status transitions, authentication-reference linking/unlinking, trust-reference assignment); `CustomerIdentityId`, `IdentityStatus` (with its full transition table), `AuthenticationReference`, `TrustReference` value objects; `IdentityDomainError` with 10 named factory functions, each mapped onto the existing closed 14-category `ErrorCategory` enum; and the 9 domain events this scope defines, built on the existing shared `DomainEvent<T>`/`buildEventType` contract.
- **Zero Firebase dependency, machine-enforced:** a new scoped `eslint.config.js` rule (`no-restricted-imports` for `functions/src/domains/identity/**/*.ts`) forbids `firebase-admin`/`firebase-functions` imports, mirroring the existing `apps/web` Sentry-isolation precedent. `IdentityDomainError` was defined independently of the existing `DomainCommandError` (`commandDispatcher.ts`) specifically because importing it would transitively pull in `firebase-functions/logger` — a genuine architectural finding from this task's own pre-edit analysis, not assumed.
- **Test-first throughout:** 7 test files, 68 tests, each written and confirmed failing (module-not-found or assertion failure) before its corresponding implementation. `Recovered` was confirmed (per `ENG-P2-GATE-001`) not to require a status-enum member — the status model contains exactly `registered | active | dormant | suspended | locked | closed | archived`.
- **Design choices made and disclosed, not silently invented:** `suspended`/`locked` resolve only to `active` or `closed` (no lateral movement) — a minimal, explicitly-flagged domain-foundation decision, not asserted by any governing document; the real-world authority to trigger that resolution is deferred to a future service-level policy. Registration emits only `CustomerIdentityRegistered` (not a redundant `Activated`); `Activated` is reserved for genuine reactivation from `dormant`/`suspended`/`locked`.
- **Full validation suite:** `pnpm -r run build`/`typecheck` clean; `eslint .` zero findings repo-wide; `prettier --check .` clean; `pnpm -r run test` — `functions` 27 files/162 tests passed (94 pre-existing + 68 new), `apps/web` 30 files/259 tests passed, unaffected.
- **Files created:** 7 source modules + 7 test files under `functions/src/domains/identity/`, `functions/src/domains/identity/README.md`, [`ENG-P2-001-01-implementation-report-2026-08-02.md`](../05-implementation/reports/ENG-P2-001-01-implementation-report-2026-08-02.md). **Files modified:** `eslint.config.js` (one new scoped block); `ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001`, Engineering Implementation Programme, Coding-Agent Prompt Register (narrow `ENG-P2-001-01`-only status notes — `-02` through `-10`, `ENG-P2-001` as a whole, and Capability 2 overall unchanged). No historical report, Decision Register entry, or unrelated application file modified.
- **Dependencies added:** none. **Configuration changes:** the one eslint rule above; no `package.json`/`tsconfig.json`/`vitest.config.ts` change.
- **Risks:** the aggregate's five-operation API shape is this package's own design choice, not yet validated against real persistence/command-handler constraints (`ENG-P2-001-05`/`-06`'s own future concern); the `IdentityDomainError`→`DomainCommandError`/`PlatformErrorResponse` adapter is unimplemented, deferred to a future API-layer task.
- **Rollback:** `git revert` of this task's commit(s), or discard the branch — not yet merged. Purely additive; no existing file's behavior changed; no data, deployment, or live configuration affected.

## 2026-08-04 — ENG-P2-001-03: Loyalty Number Service Foundation (application code, TDD)

- **Context:** following the Founder-authorized merge of `ENG-P2-001-01` (PR #57, merge commit `fc6e7df53f326c172e85c9e3a2c212cb16835bab`), the Founder requested implementation of `ENG-P2-001-03` — the Loyalty Number domain-foundation layer defined by `DEC-DATA-007`, `ENG-P2-ARCH-001`, and `ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001`. Worked from a clean isolated worktree/branch off `origin/main` at the exact PR #57 merge commit; the dirty primary checkout was inspected only, never modified.
- **Implemented at a new sibling domain module `functions/src/domains/loyaltyNumber/`:** the `LoyaltyNumber` value object (confirmed baseline format `ABC-234` only — 3 letters excluding `I`/`O`, 3 digits excluding `0`/`1`; canonical stored form unformatted, hyphen presentation-only; deferred `ABC-234-X` explicitly rejected); `LoyaltyNumberDomainError` with 6 factories mapped onto the existing closed 14-category `ErrorCategory` enum; `LoyaltyNumberCandidateGenerator`/`LoyaltyNumberUniquenessPort` provider-neutral ports (interfaces only, no Firestore implementation); `issueLoyaltyNumber` — the bounded issuance service (idempotent short-circuit on an existing assignment, bounded collision-retry, deterministic exhaustion failure); and the 3 domain events this scope defines (`LoyaltyNumberIssued`, `LoyaltyNumberIssuanceCollisionDetected`, `LoyaltyNumberIssuanceFailed`), built on the existing shared `DomainEvent<T>`/`buildEventType` contract.
- **Zero Firebase dependency, machine-enforced:** a new scoped `eslint.config.js` rule (`no-restricted-imports` for `functions/src/domains/loyaltyNumber/**/*.ts`) forbids `firebase-admin`/`firebase-functions` imports, mirroring `ENG-P2-001-01`'s identical precedent.
- **Test-first throughout:** 4 test files, 39 tests, each written and confirmed failing (module-not-found or assertion failure) before its corresponding implementation, including a genuine test-driven correction mid-cycle (reusing identity's own `createCustomerIdentityId` validator initially leaked an `IdentityDomainError` across the domain boundary — caught by a failing test, fixed by wrapping it into `LoyaltyNumberDomainError`).
- **Design choice made and disclosed, not silently invented:** `DEC-DATA-007` specifies only a qualitative "small maximum-retry count" for collision handling, with no exact figure anywhere in the repository (confirmed by full-text search) — `MAX_ISSUANCE_ATTEMPTS = 5` was chosen as an engineering-judgment implementation parameter (not a policy decision), with rationale grounded in the governing decision package's own collision-probability table (≈1.7×10⁻⁷ chance of 5 consecutive collisions at the package's own worst-case 1,000,000-customer rate), disclosed in full in the implementation report rather than assumed silently.
- **Full validation suite:** `pnpm -r run build`/`typecheck` clean; `eslint .` zero findings repo-wide; `prettier --check .` clean; `functions` 31 files/201 tests passed (94 pre-existing + 68 identity + 39 new), `apps/web` 30 files/259 tests passed, unaffected.
- **Files created:** 7 source modules + 4 test files under `functions/src/domains/loyaltyNumber/`, `functions/src/domains/loyaltyNumber/README.md`, [`ENG-P2-001-03-implementation-report-2026-08-04.md`](../05-implementation/reports/ENG-P2-001-03-implementation-report-2026-08-04.md). **Files modified:** `eslint.config.js` (one new scoped block); `ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001`, Engineering Implementation Programme, Coding-Agent Prompt Register (narrow `ENG-P2-001-03`-only status notes — `-01`, `-02`, `-04` through `-10`, `ENG-P2-001` as a whole, and Capability 2 overall unchanged). ## 2026-08-04 — ENG-P2-001-04: QR Identity Service Foundation (application code, TDD)

- **Context:** following the Founder-authorized merge of `ENG-P2-001-03` (PR #58, merge commit `5fb91ab7c6ade5db54f82d492182bf794ec72c04`), the Founder requested implementation of `ENG-P2-001-04` — the QR Identity domain-foundation layer defined by `DEC-DATA-007`'s QR Generation Principles and `ENG-P2-ARCH-001` §5. Worked from a clean isolated worktree/branch off `origin/main` at the exact PR #58 merge commit; the dirty primary checkout was inspected only, never modified.
- **Implemented at a new sibling domain module `functions/src/domains/qrIdentity/`:** the `QrReference` value object (opaque safe-charset token, no invented length/entropy policy — none is governed) and `QrPayload` (single-field wrapper, structurally incapable of carrying name/phone/email/trust/auth/reward/purchase data); `QrIdentityDomainError` with 6 factories mapped onto the existing closed 14-category `ErrorCategory` enum; the provider-neutral `QrReferenceGenerator` port (no Firebase/browser/QR-image-library dependency); three pure lifecycle functions (`issueQrIdentity`, `regenerateQrIdentity`, `restoreQrIdentityForRecovery`) matching `ENG-P2-ARCH-001` §5's named phases exactly; and 3 domain events (`QrIdentityIssued`, `QrIdentityInvalidated`, `QrIdentityRegenerated`).
- **A genuine textual tension identified and reconciled, disclosed rather than silently resolved:** `ENG-P2-ARCH-001` §5 says regeneration leaves "the underlying `qrReference`/Loyalty Number relationship... unchanged," while `ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001`'s own `-04` section (in three separate places within the same section) requires "a new resolvable reference" with "the prior reference fail[ing] to resolve." Read "relationship unchanged" as the stable mapping (which identity/loyalty number this QR represents), not literal identity of the reference string — regeneration therefore produces a genuinely new reference while identity/loyalty-number permanence is preserved. Full analysis in the implementation report §6.
- **Zero Firebase dependency, machine-enforced:** a new scoped `eslint.config.js` rule (`no-restricted-imports` for `functions/src/domains/qrIdentity/**/*.ts`) forbids `firebase-admin`/`firebase-functions` imports, mirroring `-01`/`-03`'s identical precedent.
- **Test-first throughout:** 5 test files, 39 tests, each written and confirmed failing before its corresponding implementation.
- **Scope decisions disclosed, not silently invented:** no separate "invalid payload" error (the approved payload is only the reference, so payload validation collapses into reference validation — an artificial third error would never trigger); no uniqueness port (the task's own Required Scope list, unlike Loyalty Number's, does not ask for one — collision-checking is deferred to the persistence layer); no separate "retired" lifecycle status (`ENG-P2-ARCH-001` §3 names QR-field retention on closure without naming a distinct QR-domain retired state — two statuses, `active`/`invalidated`, satisfy retention-without-reuse structurally).
- **Full validation suite:** `pnpm -r run build`/`typecheck` clean; `eslint .` zero findings repo-wide; `prettier --check .` clean; `functions` 36 files/240 tests passed (201 pre-existing + 39 new), `apps/web` 30 files/259 tests passed, unaffected.
- **Files created:** 7 source modules + 4 test files under `functions/src/domains/qrIdentity/`, `functions/src/domains/qrIdentity/README.md`, [`ENG-P2-001-04-implementation-report-2026-08-04.md`](../05-implementation/reports/ENG-P2-001-04-implementation-report-2026-08-04.md). **Files modified:** `eslint.config.js` (one new scoped block); `ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001`, Engineering Implementation Programme, Coding-Agent Prompt Register (narrow `ENG-P2-001-04`-only status notes — `-01`–`-03`, `-05` through `-10`, `ENG-P2-001` as a whole, and Capability 2 overall unchanged). No historical report, Decision Register entry, or unrelated application file modified.
- **Dependencies added:** none. **Configuration changes:** the one eslint rule above; no `package.json`/`tsconfig.json`/`vitest.config.ts` change.
- **Risks:** none beyond the disclosed textual reconciliation above, which is fully documented and reversible if a future reviewer reads the governing text differently.
- **Rollback:** `git revert` of this task's commit(s), or discard the branch — not yet merged. Purely additive; no existing file's behavior changed; no data, deployment, or live configuration affected.

## 2026-08-04 — ENG-P2-001-04 Correction: QR Regeneration Wording Clarification (Founder Review, PR #59 held)

- **Context:** Founder review of PR #59 withheld merge authorisation pending clarification of the documented tension between `ENG-P2-ARCH-001` §5's Regeneration row ("relationship unchanged") and `ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001`'s `-04` section requirements ("new resolvable reference," "prior reference fails to resolve"). A focused 8-point analysis was required before any change.
- **Finding:** not a cross-document conflict — `ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001`'s `-04` section lists `ENG-P2-ARCH-001` §5 as its own "Governing requirement," so the tension is internal to `ENG-P2-ARCH-001` §5 itself, between its own Regeneration row and its own Invalidation row ("old codes must fail closed"). That Invalidation requirement is unimplementable if the reference literally never changes, so "relationship unchanged" is the only internally-consistent reading once read as the customer↔identity↔loyalty-number **association** persisting, not literal identity of the reference **value** — which is exactly what regeneration replaces and invalidation retires.
- **Determination:** documentation correction, not a code correction, not a Founder policy decision. The implemented behaviour (`regenerateQrIdentity` issues a new reference value and marks the prior one `invalidated`; `restoreQrIdentityForRecovery` returns the current association unchanged) already matched the only internally-consistent reading — confirmed unchanged.
- **Documentation corrected (amendment-in-place, original wording preserved in git history):** `ENG-P2-ARCH-001` §5's Regeneration row; `ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001`'s `-04` Scope-bullet parenthetical and its "Updated" callout (Founder Review note appended); `ENG-P2-001-04-implementation-report-2026-08-04.md` §6 expanded with the full rigorous analysis.
- **No code or test changes.** No new dependencies. No configuration changes.
- **Validation:** targeted `qrIdentity` tests, full `functions` suite, `apps/web` suite, typecheck, lint, format, build all re-run — see the completion report for exact results and final PR head SHA.
- **Rollback:** `git revert` of this correction commit restores the pre-clarification wording (also independently recoverable from git history without a revert, since nothing was deleted).
- **Report links:** [`ENG-P2-001-04-implementation-report-2026-08-04.md`](../05-implementation/reports/ENG-P2-001-04-implementation-report-2026-08-04.md) §6.

## 2026-08-04 — ENG-P2-001-05: Customer Identity Persistence Foundation (application code, TDD)

- **Context:** following the Founder-authorized merge of `ENG-P2-001-04` (PR #59, merge commit `689a7521b5bb563dc47ef66c3b442278b63c8ff0`), the Founder requested implementation of `ENG-P2-001-05` — the Firestore-backed persistence layer for the three already-merged domain foundations (`-01`, `-03`, `-04`), required to prove nine specific invariants against the real Firebase Emulator Suite. Worked from a clean isolated worktree/branch off `origin/main` at the exact PR #59 merge commit; the dirty primary checkout was inspected only, never modified.
- **Implemented at `functions/src/domains/*/repositories/` and `functions/src/security/`:** Firestore converters (`userDocument`, `customerProfileDocument`, `loyaltyNumberDocument`, `qrIdentityRecordDocument`) and transactional repositories (`customerIdentityRepository`, `loyaltyNumberRepository`, `qrIdentityRepository`) for the `users`, `customerProfiles`, `loyaltyNumbers`, `qrIdentityRecords` collections. Global uniqueness for Loyalty Numbers and QR references via doc-ID-as-value (mirroring the existing `checkAndReserveIdempotencyKey` pattern); the full atomic QR regeneration transaction (old record invalidated with `replacedByReference`, new record active, Customer Identity ID and Loyalty Number unchanged, no partial state survives a failed transaction — proven with an emulator test); idempotent issuance for all three operations reusing the existing shared `checkAndReserveIdempotencyKey`/`completeIdempotencyKey`/`failIdempotencyKey` convention, not a competing framework. Two new concrete generator implementations (`RandomLoyaltyNumberCandidateGenerator`, `RandomQrReferenceGenerator`) — the persistence-layer responsibility each port's own doc comment had already named. Firestore Rules extended with four collection-scoped deny blocks (`users`, `customerProfiles`, `loyaltyNumbers`, `qrIdentityRecords`) — deliberately no allow branch; no direct customer read is granted because no UI consumer of this data exists anywhere in the codebase yet (all deferred). First Rules tests in this repository (`functions/src/security/firestoreRules.emulator.test.ts`, 23 tests, using the new `@firebase/rules-unit-testing` devDependency) — mutation-tested by temporarily loosening the `users` rule and confirming 5 tests failed as expected, then restoring and re-verifying green.
- **A genuine cross-cutting finding, caught by TDD and fixed narrowly:** `issueLoyaltyNumber`/`regenerateQrIdentity` (both already-merged, `-03`/`-04`) reuse the same caller-supplied `eventId` across every event a single call returns. `writeOutboxEntry` (ENG-P1-002, shared) keys the outbox document by `event.eventId`, so writing a multi-event batch (a collision-plus-issued pair, or an invalidated-plus-regenerated pair) unchanged inside one transaction let the second write silently overwrite the first. Caught by the loyalty-number collision-retry emulator test. Fixed at the repository layer only, in both `loyaltyNumberRepository.ts` and `qrIdentityRepository.ts`: when more than one event is returned, the *outbox document id* is suffixed with an index; the event's own stored `eventId` field is left untouched. The two domain services themselves were not modified.
- **A pre-existing eslint-scope gap, found and corrected:** the Firebase-isolation `no-restricted-imports` rule (`eslint.config.js`, one block per domain from `-01`/`-03`/`-04`) matched each domain folder recursively, including the new `repositories/` subfolder this task is the first to populate — even though each rule's own error message already named `-05` as where persistence-layer mapping belongs. Added a narrow `ignores: ["functions/src/domains/<domain>/repositories/**"]` to each of the three existing blocks; `models/`/`services/` remain fully restricted.
- **A required test-infrastructure correction:** `functions/vitest.emulator.config.ts` needed `fileParallelism: false`. Multiple emulator test files (three pre-existing plus four new) intentionally reset the shared `idempotencyRecords`/`outboxEntries` collections in their own `beforeEach`; running files in parallel (the prior default) let one file's cleanup delete records another file's in-flight test still depended on — reproduced, fixed, and the full 7-file/74-test emulator suite re-verified green together.
- **Full validation suite:** `pnpm -r run build`/`typecheck` clean; `eslint .` zero findings repo-wide (after the eslint-scope correction above, plus removing one genuinely-unused `expect` import); `prettier --check .` clean (after `prettier --write` on 8 files flagged for drift); `pnpm -r run test` — `functions` 42 files/275 tests passed (240 pre-existing + 35 new), `apps/web` 30 files/259 tests passed, unaffected; `pnpm emulators:validate` — 7 files/74 tests passed (23 pre-existing + 51 new); two pre-existing, already-known-flaky concurrency race tests timed out on one run and passed cleanly on an immediate re-run with no code change (same disclosed class as PR #59's own CI flakiness, confirmed unrelated to this task's diff via `git diff --name-only`).
- **Files created:** `functions/src/domains/{identity,loyaltyNumber,qrIdentity}/repositories/` (converters + repositories + tests, 8 source files + 8 test files), two new generator implementations + tests, `functions/src/security/firestoreRules.emulator.test.ts`, [`ENG-P2-001-05-implementation-report-2026-08-04.md`](../05-implementation/reports/ENG-P2-001-05-implementation-report-2026-08-04.md). **Files modified:** `eslint.config.js` (3 `ignores` additions); `functions/vitest.emulator.config.ts` (`fileParallelism: false`); `firestore.rules` (4 new deny blocks); `functions/package.json`/`pnpm-lock.yaml` (`@firebase/rules-unit-testing`, `firebase` devDependencies); `identityErrors.ts`/`.test.ts`, `loyaltyNumberErrors.ts`/`.test.ts`, `qrIdentityErrors.ts`/`.test.ts` (persistence-boundary factories appended to each existing error class — no new hierarchy); `ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001`, Engineering Implementation Programme, Coding-Agent Prompt Register (narrow `ENG-P2-001-05`-only status notes — `-01`, `-03`, `-04` remain implemented-pending-merge as previously recorded; `-02`, `-06` through `-10`, `ENG-P2-001` as a whole, and Capability 2 overall unchanged). No historical report, Decision Register entry, or unrelated application file modified.
- **Dependencies added:** `@firebase/rules-unit-testing` (devDependency) and its `firebase` peer dependency (devDependency, version-matched to `apps/web`'s existing pin — no drift). **Configuration changes:** the eslint/vitest-emulator-config/Rules changes described above; no `tsconfig.json` change.
- **Risks:** no direct customer read path exists yet for any of the four collections — by design, but a future query/lookup task will need to open one narrowly rather than widen this task's deny posture; `restoreQrIdentityForRecovery` has no repository-layer caller yet (recovery orchestration is a future package); the two flaky concurrency tests are pre-existing, unrelated to this task's diff, and already disclosed once during PR #59.
- **Rollback:** `git revert` of this task's commit(s), or discard the branch — not yet merged. Purely additive; no existing file's runtime behavior changed; no data, deployment, or live configuration affected.

## 2026-08-04 — ENG-P2-001-06: Identity Lifecycle and Status Management (application code, TDD)

- **Context:** following the Founder-authorized merge of `ENG-P2-001-05` (PR #60, merge commit `49a1a2c21efc8e91823df866eaa39f7a48d7462f`), the Founder requested implementation of `ENG-P2-001-06` — the controlled lifecycle/status-management layer for Customer Identity. Worked from a clean isolated worktree/branch off `origin/main` at the exact PR #60 merge commit; the dirty primary checkout was inspected only, never modified.
- **Finding from the mandatory pre-edit analysis:** `-01`'s already-merged domain layer already implements the full required status set and transition matrix (`registered`/`active`/`dormant`/`suspended`/`locked`/`closed`/`archived`) correctly — the actual gap for this package was the application/persistence layer, plus two genuinely-missing domain events.
- **`ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001` §14 Ambiguity 1 resolved** (whether `Recovered` is a persistent status, transition, event, or marker): the ambiguity's own table already states "Founder input required? No," recommending `Recovered` be implemented as a transient transition marker — exactly what was implemented (`IdentityRecovered` domain event only, never a persisted `IdentityStatus` value), matching `-01`'s already-adopted enum (no `recovered` member). Marked resolved in the roadmap doc.
- **Implemented at `functions/src/domains/identity/{models,services,repositories}/`:** bounded `TransitionAuthority` (`customer_initiated`/`system_initiated`/`support_initiated`/`administrator_initiated`/`security_policy_initiated`) and `TransitionReason` (9 categories) value objects; `recoverCustomerIdentity` — the recovery boundary, restricted to `suspended`/`locked` source statuses only (`dormant` explicitly needs no recovery step per `ENG-P2-ARCH-001` §3), preserving Customer Identity ID/authentication references/trust reference/Loyalty Number/QR reference by construction, never creating a second identity; a new `IdentityBecameDormant` event filling the one gap `-01`'s own code comment explicitly deferred to this task, and a new `IdentityRecovered` event — both added narrowly to the existing, already-merged `identityEvents.ts`/`customerIdentity.ts` (one new `if` branch in `buildStatusEvent`, the existing `active`/`suspended`/`locked`/`closed`/`archived` branches byte-for-byte unchanged); a transactional, idempotent `identityLifecycleRepository.ts` (`transitionCustomerIdentityStatus`, `recoverCustomerIdentityStatus`) reusing `-05`'s established `checkAndReserveIdempotencyKey`/transaction pattern exactly, with genuine stale-expected-status rejection.
- **A genuine implementation gap found and fixed by TDD:** the `expectedCurrentStatus` stale-state check was declared in the repository's own params type but never actually validated in the transaction body — caught by the dedicated "rejects a stale expected-status assumption" emulator test, fixed narrowly (added the missing comparison), not weakened by loosening the test.
- **Firestore Rules: no change required.** `-05`'s existing deny-all `users/{id}` block already covers every client-mutation path this package's own security requirements name — assessed explicitly, not silently skipped.

## 2026-08-05 — ENG-P2-001-07: Identity Recovery Foundation (application code, TDD)

- **Context:** following the Founder-authorized merge of `ENG-P2-001-06` (PR #61, merge commit `d1c3155ea3a469ca7b1c793bfe689507b376ba13`), the Founder requested implementation of `ENG-P2-001-07` — the identity-owned portion of Identity Recovery. Worked from a clean isolated worktree/branch off `origin/main` at the exact PR #61 merge commit; the dirty primary checkout was inspected only, never modified.
- **Scope narrowing disclosed:** this task's own current brief defines a narrower Authentication boundary than `ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001` §2's original `-07` scope text (which described "restoring access references — linking a new/updated Authentication reference" as in-scope). The current, more specific brief explicitly excludes authentication-provider relinking, exposing only a provider-neutral handoff reference. Followed as the authoritative, current instruction — disclosed rather than silently reconciled.
- **Implemented at `functions/src/domains/identity/{models,repositories}/`:** a provider-neutral `RecoveryProof` contract (`recoveryProof.ts`) — closed 5-category proof-method enum (derived from TRD12 §12.30, excluding the Business Ownership Recovery category which is §12.32's separate concern), pure `validateRecoveryProof` rejecting missing/malformed/rejected/mismatched-target/expired proofs; a bounded, exact-match, non-enumerable identity-lookup boundary (`identityRecoveryRepository.ts`) supporting Customer Identity ID, Loyalty Number (read via `-03`'s already-exported `fromLoyaltyNumberDocument` converter, no new export added), and current QR reference (reusing `-04`'s already-exported, already-fail-closed `getActiveQrIdentityByReference` unchanged) — Authentication-subject-reference and support-managed-reference routes assessed and deliberately deferred (no reverse index exists yet without new schema); `-06`'s `recoverCustomerIdentityStatus` extended (additive) to validate the proof, atomically reserve a new `recoveryProofReferences/{proofReference}` doc-ID-keyed collection (proof-reuse prevention, mirroring the existing idempotency-key/`loyaltyNumbers`/`qrIdentityRecords` doc-ID-as-value pattern exactly — no competing framework), and forward proof evidence into the existing `IdentityRecovered` event (additively extended: `resultingStatus`, `recoveryProofReference`, `proofMethodCategory`), the same class of additive, zero-external-consumer extension already accepted once this session (PR #61's own correction).
- **Duplicate-prevention assessment:** every lookup route is inherently 1:1 (doc-ID reads cannot return multiple documents), so true "ambiguous identity match" cannot mechanically arise within this Foundation's exact-match-only design — assessed and disclosed rather than given a fabricated, untestable trigger (heuristic duplicate detection is `-08`'s explicit scope). The one real, reachable, tested condition — the proof's own target identity disagreeing with the identity a lookup reference resolves to (covering both "proof for a different identity" and "a Loyalty Number/QR reference already linked to another identity") — is implemented and tested (`recoveryProofIdentityMismatchError`).
- **Firestore Rules: no change required.** The existing deny-by-default catch-all already covers the one new collection this package introduces (`recoveryProofReferences`) — confirmed explicitly by two new targeted Rules emulator tests, not silently assumed.
- **Full validation suite:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `functions` unit 46 files/327 tests passed (310 pre-existing + 17 new); targeted new/modified emulator files 20/20 (`identityRecoveryRepository`, `identityLifecycleRepository`) plus Rules 25/25 (23 pre-existing + 2 new), run in isolation to avoid unrelated system-load-induced concurrency flakiness; full `pnpm emulators:validate` re-run multiple times under elevated host system load (confirmed via `uptime`/`ps aux`, unrelated host processes) — every observed failure was in a pre-existing, already-disclosed, unrelated concurrency-race test class, never in this task's own files.
- **Two genuine test-data bugs found and fixed during TDD (not implementation defects):** a stray duplicate re-suspend call in the reused-proof emulator test (fixed by removing it); three Loyalty Number test fixtures using disallowed characters (`O`, `0`, `1`, excluded by `DEC-DATA-007`'s format) — fixed by substituting valid values (`ABC234`/`ABD235`/`ABE236`).
- **Files created:** `functions/src/domains/identity/models/recoveryProof.ts` (+tests); `functions/src/domains/identity/repositories/identityRecoveryRepository.ts` (+emulator tests); [`ENG-P2-001-07-implementation-report-2026-08-05.md`](../05-implementation/reports/ENG-P2-001-07-implementation-report-2026-08-05.md) (also serves as this task's persistent task-level Markdown record). **Files modified (narrow, additive only):** `identityEvents.ts`/`.test.ts`, `identityLifecycleService.ts`/`.test.ts`, `identityLifecycleRepository.ts`/`.emulator.test.ts`, `identityErrors.ts`/`.test.ts`, `firestoreRules.emulator.test.ts` (no `firestore.rules` file change). No historical report, Decision Register entry, or unrelated application file modified.

## 2026-08-05 — ENG-P2-001-08: Identity Linking and Duplicate Prevention (application code, TDD)

- **Context:** following the Founder-authorized merge of `ENG-P2-001-07` (PR #62, merge commit `9bea9dbaa4b68af9736be31f725843637d0eb9c0`), the Founder requested implementation of `ENG-P2-001-08` — provider-neutral authentication-reference link/unlink orchestration with cross-identity uniqueness enforcement. Worked from a clean isolated worktree/branch off `origin/main` at the exact PR #62 merge commit; the dirty primary checkout was inspected only, never modified.
- **Architectural gap found and closed:** `-01`/`-05`'s existing `users/{id}.authenticationReferences` field is only a per-identity projection with no cross-identity uniqueness enforcement — nothing previously prevented the same authentication reference from being linked to two different identities. This task adds the missing authoritative source: a new, doc-ID-keyed `authenticationReferences/{referenceType}:{referenceId}` collection (mirroring `loyaltyNumbers/{value}`/`qrIdentityRecords/{qrReference}`'s established pattern), updated atomically alongside the existing projection in one Firestore transaction.
- **Scope narrowing disclosed:** `ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001` §2's original `-08` text frames duplicate prevention primarily as heuristic contact-attribute matching plus a support-review queue deliverable. The current, more specific Founder brief instead defines duplicate prevention structurally — refusing a second identity from linking a `(referenceType, referenceId)` pair another identity already owns. Heuristic detection and the review-queue UI remain unimplemented, disclosed as out of this task's scope rather than silently expanded to match the older planning text. `ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001` §14 Ambiguity 4 (automatic merge authority) remains untouched — this task implements detection/fail-closed refusal only, never merge execution.
- **Implemented at `functions/src/domains/identity/repositories/authenticationReferenceRepository.ts` (new):** `linkAuthenticationReferenceForIdentity` and `unlinkAuthenticationReferenceForIdentity` — both transactional and idempotent (reusing the existing `checkAndReserveIdempotencyKey`/`completeIdempotencyKey`/`failIdempotencyKey` pattern exactly). A cross-identity link conflict fails closed: the transaction commits only a privacy-safe `AuthenticationReferenceConflictDetected` outbox entry (no identity/authoritative-record mutation), and the caller then receives a typed domain error — durable audit evidence is preserved even though the command itself fails. Unlink rejects ownership mismatches as "not found" (never revealing a reference belongs to a different identity) and preserves history on the authoritative record (`status: "unlinked"`, never deleted) rather than hard-deleting it. `-01`'s `linkAuthenticationReference`/`unlinkAuthenticationReference` domain functions were extended (additive 4th parameter, `meta: LinkAuthenticationReferenceMeta`) to carry `authority`/`reason` onto their events, reusing the same closed `TransitionAuthority`/`TransitionReason` enums `-06` established.
- **New domain event:** `AuthenticationReferenceConflictDetected` — deliberately omits the owning identity's ID and the raw `referenceId`, carrying only the attempting identity's ID, provider category, and audit metadata, to avoid leaking cross-identity linkage information to future consumers (locked in by a dedicated negative test).
- **Firestore Rules: no change required.** The existing deny-by-default catch-all already covers the new collection — confirmed, not assumed, by 3 new targeted Rules tests mirroring `-07`'s `recoveryProofReferences` pattern.
- **Full validation suite:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `functions` unit 46 files/333 tests passed (327 pre-existing + 6 new); `apps/web` 30 files/259 tests passed (unchanged, no file touched); `pnpm emulators:validate` 10 files/114 tests passed (96 pre-existing + 18 new: 15 repository + 3 Rules) on the qualifying clean run — one earlier full-suite run hit a single, pre-existing, unrelated `idempotencyService.emulator.test.ts` concurrency timeout under elevated host load, confirmed transient by an isolated re-run of that file alone (7/7 passed); `pnpm build` clean for both workspaces.
- **Files created:** `functions/src/domains/identity/repositories/authenticationReferenceRepository.ts` (+`.emulator.test.ts`); [`ENG-P2-001-08-implementation-report-2026-08-05.md`](../05-implementation/reports/ENG-P2-001-08-implementation-report-2026-08-05.md) (also serves as this task's persistent task-level Markdown record). **Files modified (narrow, additive only):** `identityErrors.ts`/`.test.ts`, `identityEvents.ts`/`.test.ts`, `customerIdentity.ts`/`.test.ts`, `firestoreRules.emulator.test.ts` (no `firestore.rules` change); `ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001` (`-08`-only status/matrix notes, scope-narrowing disclosure); Engineering Implementation Programme (narrow `-08`-only note — `-01`, `-03`, `-04`, `-05`, `-06`, `-07` remain merged as previously recorded; `-02`, `-09`, `-10`, `ENG-P2-001` as a whole, and Capability 2 overall unchanged). No historical report, Decision Register entry, or unrelated application file modified.
- **Dependencies added:** none. **Configuration changes:** none.
- **Risks:** none new beyond the already-disclosed, pre-existing local emulator concurrency-flakiness class (environmental, confirmed transient, not a regression). The relink-after-unlink policy (same identity only, disclosed in the Implementation Report §3 point 10) is this task's own conservative resolution of a genuinely unresolved governing-text gap, flagged for Founder review.
- **Rollback:** `git revert` of this task's commit(s), or discard the branch — not yet merged. Purely additive; no data, deployment, or live configuration affected.

## 2026-08-05 — ENG-P2-001-08 Correction: Authentication Reference Permanence Principle Recorded (Founder Review)

- **Context:** Founder review of `PR #63` positively reviewed the implementation and withheld merge authorisation only until the disclosed relink-after-unlink policy (Implementation Report §3 point 10) was formally recorded. The Founder approved the **Authentication Reference Permanence Principle**: "Once an authentication reference has been linked to a Customer Identity, its historical ownership remains permanently associated with that identity. If unlinked, it may be restored only to the same Customer Identity. Linking it to a different identity is prohibited unless a future governed manual-review and transfer process explicitly authorises the change."
- **Policy record location:** `ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001` §14 Decision and Ambiguity Register, new **Ambiguity 5** (resolved), plus a new §4A ("Authentication Reference Permanence Principle") in the [`ENG-P2-001-08` Implementation Report](../05-implementation/reports/ENG-P2-001-08-implementation-report-2026-08-05.md), quoting the principle verbatim and distinguishing the current approved MVP policy from the deferred future manual-transfer capability.
- **Code changes: none.** The implementation already enforced same-identity relink permitted / cross-identity relink rejected / historical ownership retained / no automatic transfer or merge — confirmed by re-running the targeted repository test suite before adding any new test, and again after.
- **Tests changed:** 2 new tests added to `authenticationReferenceRepository.emulator.test.ts` — a different identity is rejected from linking a reference the original identity previously unlinked (with the authoritative record's ownership/status unchanged and a privacy-safe conflict event recorded); the original owner's relink deterministically wins a concurrent race against a different identity's link attempt after unlink. The existing "unlinks a reference and preserves its history" test was strengthened with an explicit `customerIdentityId`-unchanged assertion. All 17 tests in the file (15 pre-existing + 2 new) pass with zero production-code change.
- **Full validation suite re-run:** `pnpm lint` clean; `pnpm format:check` clean; `npx tsc --noEmit` clean; `functions` unit tests 333/333 (unchanged — no unit-test-level file touched by this correction); `apps/web` unit tests 259/259 (unchanged); `pnpm emulators:validate` (real Firebase Emulator Suite) 10 files/116 tests passed (114 pre-existing + 2 new); `pnpm build` clean for both workspaces.
- **Files modified:** `authenticationReferenceRepository.emulator.test.ts` (+2 tests, 1 strengthened assertion); `ENG-P2-001-08-implementation-report-2026-08-05.md` (new §4A, §3 point 10 marked resolved, §6/§15 updated); `ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001` (new §14 Ambiguity 5, §2 `-08` blockquote updated to record Founder review); this entry; `docs/00-governance/documentation-changes-log.md` (correction entry). No unrelated file modified; no application logic changed.
- **Dependencies added:** none. **Configuration changes:** none.
- **Risks:** none. This correction only records an already-enforced policy and strengthens test coverage; no behavior changed.
- **Rollback:** `git revert` of this correction's commit, or discard the branch — not yet merged. Purely additive (tests + documentation only).

## 2026-08-05 — ENG-P2-001-09: Identity Query and Lookup Interfaces (application code, TDD)

- **Context:** following the Founder-authorized merge of `ENG-P2-001-08` (PR #63, merge commit `35d714724c9dfd38079e2aa586cf6e604d7ebd0f`), the Founder requested implementation of `ENG-P2-001-09` — the bounded, exact-match-only Identity Query and Lookup layer. Worked from a clean isolated worktree/branch off `origin/main` at the exact PR #63 merge commit; the dirty primary checkout was inspected only, never modified.
- **Fundamental principle honoured:** "Customer Identity is permanent. Lookup is temporary." A lookup never creates, updates, merges, or recovers an identity — it only resolves an already-existing identity through one of four permanent identifiers to a bounded result, or fails closed.
- **Implemented at `functions/src/domains/identity/repositories/identityLookupRepository.ts` (new):** four lookup functions — by Customer Identity ID, Loyalty Number, QR reference, Authentication Reference — each exact-match only (no fuzzy/partial/wildcard/prefix search anywhere), each gated by a caller-declared, closed `IdentityLookupPurpose` (`authentication`/`recovery`/`internal_service`/`merchant_transaction`/`support`) checked against a small hardcoded per-lookup-type allow-list (not a role/permission system), each returning a bounded `IdentityLookupResult` (`customerIdentityId`, `status`, the existing reference-only `authenticationReferences` — never phone, email, trust score, verification evidence, purchase history, or rewards). "Unknown identifier" and "identifier exists but is not currently active" (invalidated QR, unlinked Authentication Reference) deliberately collapse into one `identityLookupNotFoundError` at this boundary, hardening against enumeration beyond what the underlying `-04`/`-08` repositories provide on their own.
- **Reuse over duplication:** `getActiveQrIdentityByReference` (`-04`) reused unmodified; two small new exported functions added to existing repositories rather than new files — `getLoyaltyNumberAssignmentByValue` (`loyaltyNumberRepository.ts`) and `getActiveAuthenticationReferenceOwner` (`authenticationReferenceRepository.ts`). No identity state duplicated, no parallel index introduced, no Firestore index or Rules change required — every lookup is a doc-ID `.get()` against an already deny-by-default collection.
- **Audit:** a new `IdentityLookupAttempted` event (never carrying the raw looked-up value, phone, email, or token) is emitted for support/recovery/authentication-purpose lookups, any purpose-not-permitted rejection, and any failed QR lookup regardless of purpose — the task's own named "likely candidates." Ordinary internal-service/merchant-transaction successes are not audited.
- **Full validation suite:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `functions` unit 47 files/347 tests passed (333 pre-existing + 14 new); `apps/web` unchanged (259/259, one pre-existing unrelated timing-flake confirmed transient); `pnpm emulators:validate` 11 files/143 tests passed (116 pre-existing + 27 new: 22 in the new `identityLookupRepository.emulator.test.ts`, 2 in `loyaltyNumberRepository.emulator.test.ts`, 3 in `authenticationReferenceRepository.emulator.test.ts`) — two earlier runs hit the same pre-existing, already-disclosed `outboxProcessor.emulator.test.ts` concurrency-timeout flake under elevated host load, confirmed transient by an extended-timeout override run and two subsequent clean full runs; `pnpm build` clean for both workspaces.
- **Files created:** `functions/src/domains/identity/models/identityLookupPurpose.ts` (+tests); `functions/src/domains/identity/repositories/identityLookupRepository.ts` (+emulator tests); [`ENG-P2-001-09-implementation-report-2026-08-05.md`](../05-implementation/reports/ENG-P2-001-09-implementation-report-2026-08-05.md) (also serves as this task's persistent task-level Markdown record). **Files modified (narrow, additive only):** `identityErrors.ts`/`.test.ts`, `identityEvents.ts`/`.test.ts`, `authenticationReferenceRepository.ts`/`.emulator.test.ts`, `loyaltyNumberRepository.ts`/`.emulator.test.ts` (no `firestore.rules`, index, dependency, or config change). No historical report, Decision Register entry, or unrelated application file modified.
- **Dependencies added:** none. **Configuration changes:** none.
- **Risks:** none new beyond the already-disclosed, pre-existing local emulator/frontend timing-flake classes (environmental, confirmed transient, not regressions). The purpose-to-lookup-type allow-list is this task's own disclosed design resolution of the brief's "assess support for X context" instruction, flagged for Founder awareness.

## 2026-08-05 — ENG-P2-001-09 Correction: Lookup-Purpose and Result-Minimisation Clarification (Founder Review, PR #64 held)

- **Context:** Founder review of `PR #64` positively reviewed the implementation but withheld merge authorisation pending clarification that the original per-lookup-type purpose allow-list was an undisclosed engineering judgment rather than an approved policy, and that the shared `IdentityLookupResult` exposed `authenticationReferences` — linked-provider metadata — to purposes with no legitimate need for it, most critically `merchant_transaction`.
- **Final purpose policy (Founder-directed, verbatim intent):** Customer Identity ID — trusted internal, support, governed recovery only. Loyalty Number — trusted internal, governed recovery, support, authenticated participating-business transaction. Active QR — trusted internal, authenticated participating-business transaction, plus governed recovery (unchanged); `support` removed (no governing citation). Authentication Reference — Authentication, governed recovery (newly granted at the lookup level only — does not wire into `-07`'s own unmodified recovery orchestration), support, trusted internal. Caller-supplied purpose values remain never authority by themselves; a future trusted application boundary must verify caller entitlement and fail closed.
- **Policy record location:** `ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001` §14 Decision and Ambiguity Register, new **Ambiguity 6** (resolved), plus a new §38 ("Correction — Founder Review: Lookup-Purpose and Result-Minimisation Clarification") in the [`ENG-P2-001-09` Implementation Report](../05-implementation/reports/ENG-P2-001-09-implementation-report-2026-08-05.md), which also supersedes §6 and §11's original tables/type in place.
- **Result-model correction:** `IdentityLookupResult.authenticationReferences` changed from always-present to optional, populated only when `purpose === "authentication"` — the smallest correction satisfying both "every lookup returns only the minimum fields required for its purpose" and "merchant transaction lookups must not return Authentication References," applied uniformly across all four lookup types rather than as per-type exceptions.
- **Code changes:** `functions/src/domains/identity/repositories/identityLookupRepository.ts` — `IdentityLookupResult.authenticationReferences` made optional; `LOOKUP_PURPOSE_ALLOW_LISTS` updated (removed `support` from `qr_reference`, added `recovery` to `authentication_reference`); `toLookupResult` now accepts `purpose` and populates `authenticationReferences` only for the `authentication` purpose; all four call sites updated to pass `params.purpose`; doc comment extended with the finalized policy and its governing-evidence citations.
- **Tests changed:** `identityLookupRepository.emulator.test.ts` — 4 existing tests updated to assert purpose-scoped `authenticationReferences` presence/absence (including a `keys.not.toContain` check on the merchant-transaction paths); 3 new tests added — caller-identity irrelevance (same purpose, different `actor.actorId`, identical outcome), QR-reference `support`-purpose rejection, and Authentication-Reference `recovery`-purpose permission (succeeds, `authenticationReferences` still `undefined` since `recovery` ≠ `authentication`). 25/25 pass in the file.
- **Full validation suite re-run:** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean; `pnpm lint` clean; `pnpm format:check` clean; `functions` unit tests 347/347 (unchanged — this correction only touches emulator-tested repository code); real Firebase Emulator Suite 11 files/146 tests passed, clean first run, no flakes; `apps/web` unit tests 259/259 (unchanged, `apps/web` untouched); `pnpm build` clean for both workspaces.
- **Files modified:** `identityLookupRepository.ts`; `identityLookupRepository.emulator.test.ts`; `ENG-P2-001-09-implementation-report-2026-08-05.md` (new §38, §6/§11 marked superseded); `ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001-customer-identity-decomposition-plan.md` (new §14 Ambiguity 6, `-09` blockquote updated to record the correction); this entry; `docs/00-governance/documentation-changes-log.md` (Entry 067). No unrelated file modified.
- **Dependencies added:** none. **Configuration changes:** none.
- **Risks:** none. This correction narrows an already-bounded result and tightens a purpose allow-list to the Founder's own directed policy; no new capability surface introduced beyond the explicitly-authorized `recovery` grant on Authentication Reference lookup.
- **Rollback:** `git revert` of this correction's commit, or discard the branch — not yet merged. Purely additive/narrowing (code + tests + documentation); no data, deployment, or live configuration affected.

## 2026-08-05 — ENG-P2-001-10: Identity Audit and Observability Foundation (application code, TDD)

- **Context:** following the Founder-authorized merge of `ENG-P2-001-09` (PR #64, merge commit `924fb3beaf33df04baf3bf28d1e0e8319caf462e`), the Founder requested implementation of `ENG-P2-001-10` — the bounded Identity Audit and Observability Foundation, consolidating audit/observability behaviour across the completed Customer Identity packages. Worked from a clean isolated worktree/branch off `origin/main` at the exact PR #64 merge commit; the dirty primary checkout was inspected only, never modified.
- **Zero new domain events:** every material event named in this task's own brief already exists in `-01`/`-03`–`-09`'s merged code, confirmed by direct inspection — "identity reactivated" is the existing `customer_identity_activated` event distinguished by `previousStatus`, not a separate event. This package is a bounded read-side projection, privacy classification, query, and observability layer over the already-governed outbox — not a second event or outbox system.
- **Implemented at `functions/src/domains/identityAudit/` (new domain):** `models/auditEnvelope.ts` — `IdentityAuditRecord`/`toIdentityAuditRecord`, a pure projection over the existing `OutboxEntry`/`DomainEvent` shapes, deliberately excluding the outbox's own internal processing-state fields; `models/auditPrivacyClassification.ts` — reuses TRD21 §21.6's governed 5-class Personal Data Classification verbatim; `models/auditQueryAuthority.ts` — closed 5-value `AuditQueryAuthority` (internal_service/support/administrator/security_review/compliance_review), validated but never trusted as proof, mirroring `-09`'s `IdentityLookupPurpose` pattern; `models/identityAuditErrors.ts` — new `IdentityAuditDomainError`, reusing the same closed 14 `ErrorCategory` values; `repositories/identityAuditQueryRepository.ts` — four bounded, paginated, privacy-safe query functions over the existing `outboxEntries` collection (by Customer Identity ID, correlation ID, event type, and event ID — the last serving as this package's disclosed practical stand-in for "command ID," since no true command ID exists on `DomainEvent` anywhere in this codebase today); `observability/identityOperationalSignals.ts` — `emitIdentityOperationalSignal`, an 11-signal closed vocabulary reusing the existing backend structured logger (`shared/logging/logger.ts`), not the frontend-only Sentry adapter and not a new framework.
- **Outbox integrity:** added one new test to the existing `outboxProcessor.emulator.test.ts` (no new test framework) confirming `event` content is byte-identical before and after success/retry/dead-letter transitions — "processing status does not overwrite audit content" was already true of the existing implementation, now directly asserted. Malformed-outbox-record handling (skip, don't crash) is exercised at the new audit-query layer.
- **Firestore:** 4 new composite indexes added to `firestore.indexes.json` (event.aggregateId/correlationId/eventType/eventId, each with createdAt descending) — genuinely required, since these are the first equality-filter-plus-range Firestore queries in the codebase (every prior repository query is a plain doc-ID `.get()`); the local Emulator Suite does not enforce composite-index requirements, so all query tests passed without them, which is expected emulator behaviour, not evidence they're unneeded. No Rules change — the existing deny-by-default catch-all already covers `outboxEntries` for direct clients; every new query is server-side (Admin SDK).
- **Disclosed gaps found, correctly not fixed (each would require a cross-domain refactor forbidden by this task's own scope):** `causationId` exists on the shared `DomainEvent` type but is never populated anywhere; `shared/events/eventNaming.ts`'s `parseEventType`/`isValidEventType` regex only matches camelCase event names, but every real identity/loyaltyNumber/qrIdentity event name is snake_case, so it silently fails to parse any event type this domain actually emits (the new privacy classifier avoids depending on it). No numeric retention period is governed anywhere in TRD21/TRD10 for identity audit events — no deletion/archival job implemented, per explicit instruction not to invent one.
- **Full validation suite:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `functions` unit 52 files/384 tests passed (347 pre-existing + 37 new); `apps/web` unchanged (259/259); `pnpm emulators:validate` 12 files/159 tests passed (146 pre-existing + 13 new) — one transient failure on a first attempt (this repository's already-disclosed concurrency-test flakiness pattern under host load), a clean immediate retry passed 159/159 with zero code changes; `pnpm build` clean for both workspaces.
- **Files created:** `functions/src/domains/identityAudit/models/{auditEnvelope,auditPrivacyClassification,auditQueryAuthority,identityAuditErrors}.ts` (+ 4 `.test.ts`); `functions/src/domains/identityAudit/observability/identityOperationalSignals.ts` (+ `.test.ts`); `functions/src/domains/identityAudit/repositories/identityAuditQueryRepository.ts` (+ `.emulator.test.ts`); [`ENG-P2-001-10-implementation-report-2026-08-05.md`](../05-implementation/reports/ENG-P2-001-10-implementation-report-2026-08-05.md) (also serves as this task's persistent task-level Markdown record). **Files modified:** `firestore.indexes.json` (populated from empty); `functions/src/shared/outbox/outboxProcessor.emulator.test.ts` (+1 test, no production code change). No identity/loyaltyNumber/qrIdentity domain source file modified — this package is additive/read-only over that layer.
- **Dependencies added:** none. **Configuration changes:** `firestore.indexes.json` populated (not deployed — no `firebase deploy` run).
- **Risks:** none new. Unbounded audit-history growth given no governed retention period — flagged, not addressed, per explicit instruction.
- **Rollback:** `git revert` of this task's commit(s), or discard the branch — not yet merged. Purely additive; no existing exported function's behavior changed; no data, deployment, or live configuration affected.
- **Report links:** [`ENG-P2-001-10-implementation-report-2026-08-05.md`](../05-implementation/reports/ENG-P2-001-10-implementation-report-2026-08-05.md).

## 2026-08-05 — ENG-P2-001-10 Correction: Audit Read-Model Minimisation (Founder Review, PR #65 held)

- **Context:** Founder review of `PR #65` positively reviewed the implementation but withheld merge authorisation on one confirmed defect: `toIdentityAuditRecord` passed `event.payload` straight through to audit-query callers, so a Loyalty Number/QR reference/Authentication subject reference/recovery proof reference issued or linked via a material identity event was returned raw by `queryAuditRecordsByEventType`/`queryAuditRecordsByCustomerIdentityId`/etc.
- **Audit Read-Model Minimisation Principle (Founder-directed):** the underlying domain events and outbox records retain governed operational identifiers required for processing and immutable audit evidence, unchanged. Audit-query results must not expose raw Loyalty Numbers, QR references, Authentication subject references, phone numbers, emails, credentials, tokens, OTPs, or other sensitive/customer-linked identifiers unless a future explicitly governed audit purpose requires them — no masking, truncation, or unsalted hashing substitutes for omission, since each identifier space is enumerable.
- **Final audit projection model:** new `functions/src/domains/identityAudit/models/auditPayloadProjection.ts` — `projectAuditPayload(eventType, rawPayload)`, an explicit per-event-name allow-list, never a pass-through. Unrecognised event types fail closed to `{ payloadOmitted: true }`.
- **Event-by-event payload mapping:** see [Implementation Report §38.3](../05-implementation/reports/ENG-P2-001-10-implementation-report-2026-08-05.md#383-event-by-event-payload-mapping-the-audit-payload-catalogue) for the full 15-row catalogue. In summary: Loyalty Number/QR issuance-family events now project to no payload fields at all (the settled identifier is fully omitted); Authentication Reference linked/unlinked project `referenceType`/`authority`/`reason` only (the provider subject reference omitted); recovery projects everything except `recoveryProofReference`; ordinary lifecycle-transition events (already free of raw identifiers) are unaffected in substance, just now routed through the same explicit allow-list.
- **Code changes:** `auditEnvelope.ts` — `payload: event.payload` replaced with `payload: projectAuditPayload(event.eventType, event.payload)`; `IdentityAuditRecord.payload` retyped from `unknown` to `AuditSafePayload`. `auditPrivacyClassification.ts` — `extractEventName` exported for reuse by the new projection module rather than duplicated.
- **Tests changed:** `auditPayloadProjection.test.ts` (new, 10 tests); `auditEnvelope.test.ts` (existing baseline test's expected payload updated, 1 new integration test); `identityAuditQueryRepository.emulator.test.ts` (new 5-test block proving no raw Loyalty Number/QR reference/Authentication subject reference/recovery proof reference reaches an audit-query result while the underlying stored outbox document still carries it, plus a fail-closed unknown-event-type test). Two test-authoring bugs (missing `aggregateId` overrides, causing hard failures and one vacuous pass) were caught and fixed during this correction — every new test now asserts a non-zero, exact record count before asserting on content.
- **Full validation suite re-run:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `pnpm lint` clean; `pnpm format:check` clean; `functions` unit tests 395/395 (385 pre-existing + 10 new); real Firebase Emulator Suite 164/164 across 12 files (159 pre-existing + 5 new), clean first run; `apps/web` 259/259 (one pre-existing, already-disclosed timing flake confirmed transient on retry, `apps/web` untouched by this correction); `pnpm build` clean for both workspaces.
- **Files modified:** `auditEnvelope.ts`; `auditEnvelope.test.ts`; `auditPrivacyClassification.ts`; `identityAuditQueryRepository.emulator.test.ts`; `ENG-P2-001-10-implementation-report-2026-08-05.md` (new §38, §9 marked superseded); this entry; `docs/00-governance/documentation-changes-log.md` (Entry 069). **Files created:** `auditPayloadProjection.ts` (+ `.test.ts`). No unrelated file modified.
- **Dependencies added:** none. **Configuration changes:** none.
- **Risks:** none. This correction narrows an already-bounded audit-query surface; the underlying stored evidence and all write-side domain logic are untouched.
- **Rollback:** `git revert` of this correction's commit, or discard the branch — not yet merged. Purely additive/narrowing (code + tests + documentation); no data, deployment, or live configuration affected.
- **Report links:** [`ENG-P2-001-10-implementation-report-2026-08-05.md`](../05-implementation/reports/ENG-P2-001-10-implementation-report-2026-08-05.md) §38.

## 2026-08-06 — ENG-P2-ARCH-REVIEW-001: Capability 2 Customer Identity Architecture Review

- **Context:** following the Founder-authorized merge of `ENG-P2-001-10`'s correction (PR #65, merge commit `bc87de7e25bbf678b1af752ebb666f84725e7a77`), the Founder requested a bounded architecture review determining whether the nine merged `ENG-P2-001` child packages (`-01`, `-03`–`-10`) operate as one coherent capability and whether targeted corrections are required before `-02` or Authentication begin. Worked from a clean isolated worktree/branch off `origin/main` at the exact PR #65 merge commit; the dirty primary checkout was inspected only, never modified.
- **Review-only task:** no application code, test code, Firestore Rule, index, or configuration was changed. `ENG-P2-001-02`, Authentication, ITM, UI, and API were not begun. No finding was implemented.
- **Evidence base:** direct reading of `ENG-P2-ARCH-001`, `ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001` §14, `ENG-P2-GATE-001`, `CDR-001`, the Decision Register (`DEC-PROD-012`, `DEC-IDENTITY-001`), TRD10 §10.6, TRD12 §12.6, the Master Workflow, the Coding-Agent Prompt Register, and the RTM; plus three parallel read-only research passes covering persistence/transactions/idempotency, events/errors/audit, and security/Rules/indexes/test-coverage across all nine merged packages — every finding independently spot-verified against live code by the reviewer (exact file:line citations) before inclusion in the report.
- **Determination:** Ready with targeted corrections. Zero P0 findings. Twelve findings classified P1 (1), P2 (5), P3 (3), informational (2, including RTM's pre-existing lack of synchronization with this capability, flagged for a future task). Highest-severity finding: `recoveryProofReferences`' first-ever write (`identityLifecycleRepository.ts:243-247`) uses `stampUpdate()` instead of `stampCreate()`, omitting `createdAt`/`createdBy`/`id`/`schemaVersion` — inconsistent with every other first-write document in the capability. Full findings register, cross-package consistency matrix, integration coverage matrix (including 3 specifically-checked end-to-end scenarios — 1 fully present, 1 partially present, 2 not found), outstanding governance register, and a proposed (not executed) 6-task correction plan are in the report.

## 2026-08-06 — ENG-P2-ARCH-CORR-001: Recovery Proof Reference Metadata Contract Correction (application code, TDD)

- **Context:** following the Founder-authorized merge of `ENG-P2-ARCH-REVIEW-001` (PR #66, merge commit `82a731da57a90a063f283564f75862edc0249ead`), the Founder requested resolution of the review's Finding F1 — `recoveryProofReferences`' first-ever write used `stampUpdate()` instead of `stampCreate()`, omitting `createdAt`/`createdBy`/`id`/`schemaVersion`. Worked from a clean isolated worktree/branch off `origin/main` at the exact PR #66 merge commit; the dirty primary checkout was inspected only, never modified.
- **Root cause:** the recovery-proof reservation write (`identityLifecycleRepository.ts:243-247`, then) was authored by analogy to the adjacent `transaction.update(ref, {...})` line immediately above it — a genuine update to the pre-existing `users/{id}` document — but the reservation write is always a first write (guarded by an existence check that already rejects any second attempt), so the correct stamp function was `stampCreate()`, not `stampUpdate()`.
- **Metadata authority analysis:** `recoveryProofReferences` is an idempotency/reservation record (a consumed-proof marker), the same architectural role as `idempotencyRecords`/`loyaltyNumbers`/`qrIdentityRecords`. No document type or converter existed for it prior to this task; nothing in the codebase reads its fields back beyond `.exists()` (confirmed by repo-wide search).
- **Fix:** new `functions/src/domains/identity/repositories/recoveryProofReferenceDocument.ts` — `toRecoveryProofReferenceDocument(proofReference, customerIdentityId, actorId)`, a `stampCreate`-based typed write-side builder mirroring the established `loyaltyNumberDocument.ts` pattern for doc-ID-as-key reservation records. Deliberately **no read-side `from...` converter** — nothing reads these fields back, and adding one would be unused code (the same defect already flagged for `customerProfileDocument.ts`'s unused converter, Finding F7). Wired into `identityLifecycleRepository.ts`'s recovery transaction in place of the raw `stampUpdate`-based object literal; no other line of the transaction changed.
- **Data/migration assessment:** no migration required. No live Firebase deploy evidence exists anywhere in git history for this capability; no repository fixtures; the emulator test's own `beforeEach` clears the `recoveryProofReferences` collection before every run. Even hypothetically, an old-shape document would still correctly satisfy the sole consumer (the `.exists()` reuse-prevention check).
- **Tests:** new `recoveryProofReferenceDocument.test.ts` (4 unit tests — doc-ID-as-proof-reference, full creation-metadata shape, null-actor handling, exact key-set with no extra fields). Three existing emulator tests in `identityLifecycleRepository.emulator.test.ts` strengthened (no new file): the successful-recovery test now asserts the exact persisted field set and every value; the duplicate-recovery test now asserts exactly one reservation document exists both before and after idempotent replay, with an unchanged `createdAt`; the ineligible-status-rejection test now asserts zero reservation documents exist after the failed transaction. RED confirmed before the fix (missing-fields diff exactly matching F1) via a real `firebase emulators:exec` run against the unfixed code.
- **Atomicity/idempotency/security unaffected in substance:** the reservation write remains inside the same transaction as the status update and outbox event write; idempotent replay still short-circuits before the transaction via `checkAndReserveIdempotencyKey`; proof-reuse prevention (`proofSnapshot.exists`) unchanged; Firestore Rules' trailing wildcard deny-all for this collection untouched, its dedicated pre-existing Rules tests pass unmodified; `proofReference` remains opaque, no raw proof material ever persisted.
- **Full validation suite:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `pnpm lint` clean; `pnpm format:check` clean; `functions` unit tests 399/399 (395 pre-existing + 4 new); real Firebase Emulator Suite 164/164 across 12 files (including the 3 strengthened tests and the pre-existing `recoveryProofReferences` Rules tests), clean first run after the fix; `apps/web` 259/259 (one pre-existing, already-disclosed lazy-route timing flake confirmed transient on retry, `apps/web` untouched by this correction); `pnpm build` clean for both workspaces.
- **Files created:** `recoveryProofReferenceDocument.ts` (+ `.test.ts`); [`ENG-P2-ARCH-CORR-001-recovery-proof-reference-metadata-correction-2026-08-06.md`](../05-implementation/reports/ENG-P2-ARCH-CORR-001-recovery-proof-reference-metadata-correction-2026-08-06.md). **Files modified:** `identityLifecycleRepository.ts`; `identityLifecycleRepository.emulator.test.ts`; `ENG-P2-ARCH-REVIEW-001-...md` (Finding F1 status only, bracket-marker convention, original text preserved); this entry; `docs/00-governance/documentation-changes-log.md` (Entry 071). No unrelated file, Rule, index, configuration, or other historical report modified. F2–F12 untouched, unaddressed by design.
- **Programme/Prompt Register:** no update applied — reviewed and found to require none for this narrow correction (neither tracker's existing text claims the reservation metadata was already correct); a considered decision, recorded in the correction report §21-24.
- **Dependencies added:** none. **Configuration changes:** none.
- **Risks:** none new — purely additive/narrowing; no existing exported function's external behavior changed in substance.
- **Rollback:** `git revert` of this correction's commit, or discard the branch — not yet merged. No data, deployment, or live configuration affected either way.
- **Report links:** [`ENG-P2-ARCH-CORR-001-recovery-proof-reference-metadata-correction-2026-08-06.md`](../05-implementation/reports/ENG-P2-ARCH-CORR-001-recovery-proof-reference-metadata-correction-2026-08-06.md).

## 2026-08-06 — ENG-P2-ARCH-CORR-002: Cross-Package Identity Integration Validation (test-only, TDD)

- **Context:** following the Founder-authorized merge of `ENG-P2-ARCH-CORR-001` (PR #67, merge commit `264c29e6381e0b07a6a31ed6767bf13ad42db250`), the Founder requested resolution of the review's Finding F2 — three cross-package integration-test gaps (QR regeneration old/new-reference join, cross-package idempotent replay, audit write/read path join). Worked from a clean isolated worktree/branch off `origin/main` at the exact PR #67 merge commit; the dirty primary checkout was inspected only, never modified.
- **Test-only correction:** no production code was changed. One new dedicated emulator test file sequences real, already-merged public repository functions across all 7 architecture-review-mandated cross-package scenarios (identity issuance chain, cross-package idempotent replay, QR regeneration/lookup, lifecycle transition/audit join, recovery integration including a failed-recovery-does-not-consume-proof case, authentication-reference linking/lookup, and a real Firestore-transaction-level conflict race between recovery and closure). No test-only business logic was introduced — every scenario calls real exported functions in their governed sequence; `FixedGenerator` only injects deterministic values, never duplicates uniqueness/issuance logic.

Where no composite command exists (issuance, replay), the longest real chain of governed package commands is exercised instead, per the task's own guidance.

Full per-scenario matrix in the correction report.

One earlier full-suite run showed a single unrelated transient failure in `authenticationReferenceRepository.emulator.test.ts`'s pre-existing concurrency test (this session's already-documented flakiness pattern under host load), confirmed transient by the clean retry and untouched by this task.

## 2026-08-06 — ENG-P2-ARCH-CORR-003: Audit Projection and Lookup Atomicity Documentation Corrections (application code + docs, TDD)

- **Context:** following the Founder-authorized merge of `ENG-P2-ARCH-CORR-002` (PR #68, merge commit `9e37b4b5706c7846bcd523ff7f1b0c53ed6d2394`), the Founder requested resolution of Findings F3 (audit-catalogue gap for `trust_reference_updated`) and F4 (`identityLookupRepository.ts`'s overstated atomicity comment). Worked from a clean isolated worktree/branch off `origin/main` at the exact PR #68 merge commit; the dirty primary checkout was inspected only, never modified.
- **F3 root-cause investigation:** `buildTrustReferenceUpdatedEvent` (`identityEvents.ts:199`) is called by `setTrustReference` (`customerIdentity.ts:273-290`), a real, unit-tested domain function — but a repo-wide grep confirms no repository calls `setTrustReference`, so the event never reaches the outbox in any current execution path. This is `-01`'s own forward-defined aggregate capability for a future ITM integration, not dead code. Determination: explicit privacy-safe projection required.
- **F3 fix:** `auditPayloadProjection.ts` — added `case "trust_reference_updated": return {};`. The payload's only field, `trustRecordId`, is an opaque enumerable ITM-record reference, treated identically to `recoveryProofReference`/`referenceId` elsewhere in the catalogue (deliberately never picked). `customerIdentityId` is never itself part of any event's payload projection anywhere in this catalogue.
- **F4 root-cause investigation:** `identityLookupRepository.ts`'s header comment claimed the identity read and the audit write happen "atomically... in a Firestore transaction." Direct code trace confirmed the read is a plain, non-transactional `.get()`, fully completed before the audit write's own separate `db.runTransaction` begins. No data-integrity defect exists (a `.get()` cannot produce torn writes); the actual guarantee (for audited outcomes, a failed audit write causes the whole call to fail, so the caller never receives a "resolved" result whose audit entry doesn't exist) is real and already proven by the existing, unmodified test suite. Determination: comment-only correction, zero behaviour change.
- **F4 fix:** header comment replaced with an accurate description of the sequential-coupling consistency model. Zero code lines changed — confirmed via `git diff`, every changed line is a `*`-prefixed JSDoc comment line.
- **Tests:** one new unit test in `auditPayloadProjection.test.ts` (RED confirmed first — asserted `{}`, received `{ payloadOmitted: true }` — then GREEN after the fix). `identityLookupRepository.emulator.test.ts` deliberately not modified — its continued, unmodified pass is itself the required regression proof for F4's zero-behaviour-change claim.
- **Full validation suite:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `pnpm lint` clean; `pnpm format:check` clean; `functions` unit tests 400/400 (399 pre-existing + 1 new); real Firebase Emulator Suite 172/172 across 13 files, confirmed on two full-suite runs; `apps/web` 259/259; both workspace builds clean.
- **Files modified:** `functions/src/domains/identityAudit/models/auditPayloadProjection.ts`; `auditPayloadProjection.test.ts`; `functions/src/domains/identity/repositories/identityLookupRepository.ts` (comment only); `ENG-P2-001-10-implementation-report-2026-08-05.md` §38.3 (audit payload catalogue, new row added in place); `ENG-P2-ARCH-REVIEW-001-...md` (Findings F3/F4 status only, bracket-marker convention, original text preserved); this entry; `docs/00-governance/documentation-changes-log.md` (Entry 073). **Files created:** [`ENG-P2-ARCH-CORR-003-audit-projection-and-lookup-atomicity-corrections-2026-08-06.md`](../05-implementation/reports/ENG-P2-ARCH-CORR-003-audit-projection-and-lookup-atomicity-corrections-2026-08-06.md). No unrelated file, Rule, index, configuration, or other historical report modified. F5–F11 untouched, unaddressed by design; Customer Profile/Authentication/ITM/Capability 2 overall status unchanged.
- **Dependencies added:** none. **Configuration changes:** none.
- **Risks:** none new — F3 is additive (a new, currently-unreachable explicit case); F4 changes zero executable code.
- **Rollback:** `git revert` of this correction's commit, or discard the branch — not yet merged. Purely additive/documentation; no data, deployment, or live configuration affected either way.
